{"forms":[{"form":"splitting fields","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["(of a polynomial) Given a polynomial p over a field K, the smallest extension field L of K such that p, as a polynomial over L, decomposes into linear factors (polynomials of degree 1); (of a set of polynomials) given a set P of polynomials over K, the smallest extension field of K over which every polynomial in P decomposes into linear factors."],"qualifier":"Galois theory","synonyms":[{"word":"root field"}],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The terminology \"splitting field of a K-algebra\" is motivated by the same terminology regarding a polynomial. A splitting field of a K-algebra A is a field extension K#92;mapstoL such that A#92;otimes#95;KL is split; in the special case A#61;K#91;x#93;#47;f(x) this is the same as a splitting field of the polynomial f(x).","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Given a finite-dimensional K-algebra (algebra over a field), an extension field whose every simple (indecomposable) module is absolutely simple (remains simple after the scalar field has been extended to said extension field)."],"qualifier":"ring theory","raw_tags":["of a K-algebra"],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Every finite dimensional central simple algebra has a splitting field: moreover, if said CSA is a division algebra, then a maximal subfield of it is a splitting field.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Given a central simple algebra A over a field K, another field, E, such that the tensor product A⊗E is isomorphic to a matrix ring over E."],"qualifier":"ring theory","raw_tags":["of a central simple algebra"],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["(of a character χ of a representation of a group G) A field K over which a K-representation of G exists which includes the character χ; (of a group G) a field over which a K-representation of G exists which includes every irreducible character in G."],"qualifier":"character theory","topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":7413960,"upstream_offset":16657332344,"upstream_record_sha256":"5397fcb23d9bd096bc47a6f67efecdf2ce343cc37b37596ee729a9abf0ed6eb3","word":"splitting field"}
